if (seen) {
	$.ajax({
		url: "/en/d/recommended-operators",
		data: {
			ids: seen.map((prod) => { return prod.operatorId }).join(",")
		},
		success: function(data) {
			var template = '' +
			'<a class="foryo-item item js-foryo-item" href="%LINK%">' +
				'<div class="foryo-item__banner-wrap">' +
					'<div class="foryo-item__banner" style="background-image: url(%IMAGE%);"></div>' +
				'</div>' +
				'<h3 class="foryo-item__title">%NAME%</h3>' +
				'%RATING%' +
				'<span class="foryo-item__rating-num">%RATINGNUM%</span>' +
			'</a>';

			var output = '';
			data.recommendations.forEach(function(op) {
				var star = '<i class="foryo-item__star streamline-uniF005"></i>';
				var rating = op.rating ? star.repeat(op.rating) : '';
				var operatorBanner = op.images[0].replace('.jpg', '_desktop.jpg');

				output += template.replace('%LINK%', op.url).replace('%IMAGE%', operatorBanner).replace('%NAME%', op.name).replace('%RATING%', rating).replace('%RATINGNUM%', op.rating ? op.rating : '');

			});

			document.querySelector('.js-foryo').style.display = 'block';

			var isBigList = forYou.length > 4;

$(function() {
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "/en/d/currency",
        cache: false
    }).done(function(data) {
        $('.js-current-currency').text(data.code).attr('data-current-rate', data.rate);
        $('.js-current-currency-symbol').text(data.symbol ? '('+data.symbol+')' : '');
    });

    $(".js-currency-item").on('click', function(event) {

        event.stopImmediatePropagation();

        var currencyItem = $(this);
        if (currencyItem.hasClass('js-currency-item-selected')) return;

        // Close the options when some currency is selected
        $('.js-currency-menu').removeClass('curr-menu--active curr-menu--active-vis');
      
        var code = $(this).find('.js-currency-code').text();

        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/en/d/currency',
            cache: false,
            data: {code: code}

        }).done(function(result, status, jqXHR) {

            if (result.error) {
                console.log(result.error);
                return;
            }

            $('.js-current-currency').text(result.code).attr('data-current-rate', result.rate);
            $('.js-current-currency-symbol').text(result.symbol);
            $('html').trigger("currency-changed");
            $('.js-currency-item.js-currency-item-selected').removeClass('js-currency-item-selected');
            currencyItem.addClass('js-currency-item-selected');
        });
    });

    $('.js-currency-more').click(function() {
        this.parentElement.removeChild(this);
        $('.curr-menu__others').css('display', 'flex');
    });
});
</script><script>

	$('.js-newsletter').validate({
		errorClass: 'input__errormessage',
		validClass: 'input__wrap--confirm',

		highlight: function(element) {
			$(element).parent().addClass('input__wrap--error');
		},

		unhighlight: function(element) {
			$(element).parent().removeClass('input__wrap--error');
		},

		rules: {
            EMAIL: { required: true, email: true }
        },

        errorPlacement: function(error, element) {
		    error.insertAfter(element);
		}
	});
		
	$('.js-newsletter').submit(function(e) {
		var $this = $(this);
		$.ajax({
			type: "GET",
			url: "https://experienceoz.us12.list-manage.com/subscribe/post-json?c=?",
			data: $this.serialize(),
			dataType: 'json',
			contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
		}).done(function (result, status, jqXHR) {
			if (result.result != 'success') {
				var msg = result.msg == '0 - Please enter a value' ? '' : result.msg;
				$('.js-mc-response').html(msg);
			} else {
				$('.js-newsletter').hide();
				$('.js-exit-intent-ty').show();
			}
		}).fail(function(xhr, status) {
			console.log('Could not connect to the registration server:', status);
			document.querySelector('.js-mc-response').innerHTML = "An error has occured. Please try again later."
		});
		return false;
	});
